# Data Dictionary

Auto-generated by scripts/build_project_knowledge.py from metadata/**/*.json. Only indicators with a successful pipeline run appear here with full detail; the rest are listed at the bottom as not yet connected.

## GDP_REAL
- Name: ВВП, реальный (физический объем, методом производства) / Real GDP
- Source: bns, https://taldau.stat.gov.kz/ru/NewIndex/GetIndexTreeData
- Dataset ID: taldau-index-2979005
- Unit: index (physical volume, % of previous period)
- Frequency: annual
- Period covered: 2000-12-31 .. 2025-12-31
- Methodology: Physical volume index of GDP, production method, % of prior period (100=no change). A final-use-method variant also exists on Taldau (indexId 700974) but is not fetched here.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## GDP_NOMINAL
- Name: ВВП, номинальный / Nominal GDP
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/api/iblock/element/4439/file/ru/
- Dataset ID: GVA_NOMINAL_BY_SECTION_QUARTERLY[GDP]
- Unit: KZT
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2010-01-01 .. 2026-04-01
- Methodology: Derived from the item-level dataset GVA_NOMINAL_BY_SECTION_QUARTERLY, item GDP, scale 1000000. Year-to-date columns «1 квартал YYYYг.», «1 полугодие YYYYг.», «9 месяцев YYYYг.», «YYYY год» of the 2010–present sheet; 2010 Q1 – 2026 H1 on 2026-09-23. Same rows as GVA_NOMINAL_BY_SECTION.
- Transformation: level (year-to-date cumulative, as published -- not decumulated to discrete quarters)
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## IND_PROD
- Name: Индекс промышленного производства / Industrial Production Index
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/api/iblock/element/5792/file/ru/
- Dataset ID: INDUSTRIAL_PRODUCTION_INDEX_BY_ACTIVITY[IND]
- Unit: index, % to previous period
- Frequency: annual
- Period covered: 1990-12-31 .. 2025-12-31
- Methodology: Derived from the item-level dataset INDUSTRIAL_PRODUCTION_INDEX_BY_ACTIVITY, item IND. Codes follow ОКЭД (05, 06.1, 10.5, 24.4 …); IND is the industry total, MACH the machinery group 26–30. The scalar IND_PROD, IND_PROD_MINING/_MANUFACTURING/_ELECTRICITY series are derived from items IND, B, C, D of this dataset (the JSON cube 5809 they used to read stopped at 2023).
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## CPI
- Name: Индекс потребительских цен (инфляция) / Consumer Price Index (Inflation)
- Source: bns, https://taldau.stat.gov.kz/ru/NewIndex/GetIndexTreeData
- Dataset ID: taldau-index-703076
- Unit: index, previous month = 100
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2011-01-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Index, previous month = 100 -- 100.6 for July 2026. MOVED FROM open-data element 1549 to Taldau index 703076 on 2026-09-03: the cube stops at October 2025 (confirmed by reading its own PERIOD column, so the source and not the fetcher), while Taldau carries the same series through July 2026. The two agree to within 0.05 index points across all 178 overlapping months, so this is the same series on a channel that is still updating.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## UNEMPLOYMENT
- Name: Уровень безработицы / Unemployment Rate
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/api/iblock/element/5830/file/ru/
- Dataset ID: 5830
- Unit: % of labor force
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2001-01-01 .. 2026-04-01
- Methodology: Percent of the labour force, ILO definition, age 15+, national, quarterly from 2001Q1 (stat.gov.kz element 5830 «Основные индикаторы рынка труда», block «Уровень безработицы»). Replaced the JSON cube 102790 on 2026-09-25, which stopped at 2025Q2 and began in 2023Q1; the two agree on every shared quarter.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-25

## INVESTMENT
- Name: Инвестиции в основной капитал / Investment in Fixed Capital
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/api/iblock/element/5546/file/ru/
- Dataset ID: INVESTMENT_BY_REGION[TOTAL]
- Unit: KZT
- Frequency: annual
- Period covered: 2003-12-31 .. 2025-12-31
- Methodology: Derived from the item-level dataset INVESTMENT_BY_REGION, item TOTAL, scale 1000000. annual values from the xlsx export; quarterly year-to-date columns skipped
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## EXPORTS
- Name: Экспорт (итого) / Exports (total)
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/api/iblock/element/446905/file/ru/
- Dataset ID: EXPORTS_VALUE_BY_COMMODITY_GROUP[TOTAL]
- Unit: thousand USD
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2015-01-01 .. 2026-07-01
- Methodology: Derived from the item-level dataset EXPORTS_VALUE_BY_COMMODITY_GROUP, item TOTAL. TOTAL is the published national row; the groups are sums of 6-digit HS lines over the regional blocks. Gas condensate and the 10-digit 'основные товары' subsets (petroleum products) cannot be separated at 6 digits — CRUDE_OIL is all of 2709, PETROLEUM_PRODUCTS all of 2710. Raw file: bns_exports_2026-09-16.xlsx.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

- Name: Импорт (итого) / Imports (total)

- Source: nbk, https://data.nationalbank.kz/api/v1/data/base-rate
- Dataset ID: base-rate
- Unit: % p.a.
- Frequency: irregular
- Period covered: 2015-09-02 .. 2026-09-07
- Methodology: event-dated (rate valid until the next MPC decision), not literal daily observations

## EXCHANGE_RATE
- Name: Официальный курс USD/KZT / Official USD/KZT Exchange Rate
- Source: nbk, https://nationalbank.kz/ru/exchangerates/ezhednevnye-oficialnye-rynochnye-kursy-valyut/report
- Dataset ID: official-rates-report/USD
- Unit: KZT per 1 USD
- Frequency: daily
- Period covered: 1999-11-17 .. 2026-09-25
- Methodology: Official NBK USD/KZT rate, KZT per one USD, weekdays, from 1999-11 (the archive report's first rows, 1999-10-19..21, are malformed). Accumulated: each run reads the last 10129 days of the report and merges them with the processed history, which was loaded in full on 2026-09-25. No rate is published for 2023-07-28..2023-08-21.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-25

## GOV_DEBT

- Source: minfin, https://www.gov.kz/api/v1/public/content-manager/documents?directions=261&activities=14610
- Dataset ID: gov.kz-debt-listing
- Unit: million KZT
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2018-04-01 .. 2026-07-01
- Methodology: Built from 17 of 23 listed quarterly snapshot documents (6 skipped, ids: [260596, 80760, 80683, 52793, 47876, 29643] -- older/differently-templated files that didn't match our label-based parser; do not assume continuous quarterly coverage without checking data/unified for gaps). Values converted from the source's thousand-KZT unit to million KZT for consistency with GOV_REVENUE/GOV_EXPENDITURE.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## TONIA
- Name: Индикатор TONIA (однодневное репо с ГЦБ на KASE) / TONIA Overnight Repo Rate
- Source: kase, https://kase.kz/tv-charts/indicators/history?symbol=TONIA&resolution=D
- Dataset ID: kase-tv/TONIA
- Unit: % per annum
- Frequency: daily
- Period covered: 2001-09-02 .. 2026-09-25
- Methodology: Percent per annum. TONIA (Tenge OverNight Index Average): the weighted average rate of one-day repo transactions in government securities on KASE's automatic repo market, dated by TRADE date, from 2001-09-02 (the earliest values predate the index's formal launch and are KASE's own back-calculation). Moved on 2026-09-25 from the NBK widget (2026-02 onward, publication-dated) to KASE, the index administrator.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-25

## GS_YIELD_3M
- Name: Бескупонная доходность ГЦБ, 3 месяца (кривая KASE, среднее за месяц) / Government Securities Zero-Coupon Yield, 3M (KASE curve, monthly average)
- Source: kase, https://kase.kz/en/app-gateway/documents/curve/
- Dataset ID: kase-gs-curve/3M
- Unit: % per annum
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2019-11-01 .. 2026-09-01
- Methodology: Percent per annum, zero-coupon yield of government securities at 3M, the MONTHLY AVERAGE of KASE's daily Nelson-Siegel curves (r(t) = B0 + B1*f + B2*(f - exp(-t/TAU))), from 2019-11. The latest month (2026-09) averages the 19 curves so far. Before 2024 TAU varied from 0.08 to 5.5, so the long end of individual daily fits can be an artefact; the monthly mean damps it but does not remove it.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-25

- Dataset ID: formId=132,insurance_org_type=General,insurance_type=Total
- Unit: million KZT, year-to-date cumulative
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2023-01-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Million KZT (converted from the source's thousand-KZT unit). Total insurance premiums accepted under insurance and reinsurance contracts, GENERAL (non-life) insurance organizations. Not the same total as INSURANCE_PREMIUMS_LIFE -- the source provides no combined General+Life aggregate.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## EXTERNAL_DEBT_LONG_TERM
- Name: Внешний долг РК, долгосрочный / External Debt, Long-Term
- Source: nbk, https://data.nationalbank.kz/api/v1/data?formId=340
- Dataset ID: formId=340,class_type=Total External debt of Kazakhstan,matiruty=Long-term,period=Quarter,type= mln USD
- Unit: USD million
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2014-10-01 .. 2026-04-01
- Methodology: USD million, end of quarter. LONG-TERM portion of Kazakhstan total gross external debt. Together with EXTERNAL_DEBT_SHORT_TERM this is the maturity split behind EXTERNAL_DEBT.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## EXTERNAL_DEBT_SHORT_TERM
- Name: Внешний долг РК, краткосрочный / External Debt, Short-Term
- Source: nbk, https://data.nationalbank.kz/api/v1/data?formId=340
- Dataset ID: formId=340,class_type=Total External debt of Kazakhstan,matiruty=Short-term,period=Quarter,type= mln USD
- Unit: USD million
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2014-10-01 .. 2026-04-01
- Methodology: USD million, end of quarter. SHORT-TERM portion of Kazakhstan total gross external debt -- the rollover-risk component, and the numerator concept behind reserve-adequacy rules such as RESERVES_IMPORT_COVER.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## PRIVATE_EXTERNAL_DEBT_INTERCOMPANY

- Unit: USD million
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2014-10-01 .. 2026-04-01
- Methodology: USD million, end of quarter. Long-term private-sector external debt in the form of DIRECT INVESTMENT INTERCOMPANY LENDING -- the dominant component of Kazakhstan external debt, largely oil-sector parent-to-subsidiary financing rather than market borrowing.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## EXCHANGE_RATE_EUR_OTC

- Unit: KZT per 1 EUR
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2022-12-01 .. 2026-07-01
- Methodology: KZT per 1 EUR. Average BID rate on Kazakhstan's over-the-counter FX market. Distinct from the official NBK rate (EXCHANGE_RATE, USD only) -- this is a market average, and the form also publishes a near-identical 'Average rate - offer' series.

- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

- Unit: KZT per 1 RUB
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2022-12-01 .. 2026-07-01
- Methodology: KZT per 1 RUB. Average BID rate on Kazakhstan's over-the-counter FX market -- relevant given Russia's weight in Kazakhstan's trade and remittance flows.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## EXTERNAL_DEBT_EX_INTERCOMPANY
- Name: Внешний долг без внутрифирменного кредитования / External Debt Excluding Intercompany Lending

- Unit: USD million
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2014-04-01 .. 2026-04-01
- Methodology: USD million, end of quarter. Gross external debt EXCLUDING direct-investment intercompany lending -- the market-borrowing measure. Intercompany lending dominates Kazakhstan's headline external debt (largely oil-sector parent-to-subsidiary financing), so this is the more comparable figure.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

- Methodology: USD million, end of quarter. PUBLIC SECTOR external debt (government, central bank, and entities controlled by government). With EXTERNAL_DEBT_PRIVATE_SECTOR this sums exactly to total EXTERNAL_DEBT.

## EXTERNAL_DEBT_DUE_WITHIN_YEAR
- Name: Внешний долг к погашению в течение года / External Debt Due Within One Year (Remaining Maturity)
- Source: nbk, https://data.nationalbank.kz/api/v1/data?formId=348
- Dataset ID: formId=348,class_type=Debt,ed_code=External Debt- Reference: ED due for payment within one year,period=quarter,type=USD mln
- Unit: USD million
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2014-04-01 .. 2026-04-01
- Methodology: USD million, end of quarter. External debt DUE FOR PAYMENT WITHIN ONE YEAR on a remaining-maturity basis -- short-term debt plus the current portion of long-term debt. This is the rollover-risk measure, and is larger than EXTERNAL_DEBT_SHORT_TERM, which is on an original-maturity basis.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## RESERVES_AND_NATIONAL_FUND

- Unit: USD million
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2005-04-01 .. 2026-04-01
- Methodology: USD million, end of quarter. NBK reserve assets PLUS the foreign assets of the National Fund -- Kazakhstan's total external buffer. Held separately from FX_RESERVES (NBK reserves only, monthly) and NATIONAL_FUND_ASSETS (fund only, monthly); this is the combined figure on a balance-of-payments basis, with history back to 2005.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## STATE_DEBT_TOTAL

- Unit: million KZT
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2020-01-01 .. 2026-07-01
- Methodology: Million KZT. Section I of the bulletin's debt table: state debt (government + National Bank + local executive bodies, net of mutual claims). A COMPONENT of the existing GOV_DEBT, which is I + II + III -- verified: 38,495,061.56 + 2,414,476.28 + 6,000 = 40,915,537.84, exactly GOV_DEBT.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

- Unit: USD million
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2020-01-01 .. 2026-07-01
- Methodology: USD million. The same external government debt series as GOV_DEBT_EXTERNAL, in the source's own USD column. Consistency checked against the sheet's stated rate: 8,352,366.46 / 485.82 = 17,192.4 against the published 17,192.31.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

- Methodology: Million KZT. Transfers received by local budgets from the republican budget -- read against LOCAL_BUDGET_REVENUE this is the fiscal-dependence ratio of Kazakhstan's regions (45% of local revenue in 2025). 1 earlier point(s) restored on 2026-09-25 from bulletins archived in data/raw/minfin (no longer in the gov.kz listing).

- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

- Dataset ID: CMO-Historical-Data-Monthly.xlsx#Monthly Prices
- Unit: USD per barrel
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 1960-01-01 .. 2026-08-01

- Revision status: original

## OIL_EXPORT_DUTY
- Name: Вывозная таможенная пошлина на сырую нефть / Crude Oil Export Customs Duty
- Source: minfin, https://www.gov.kz/api/v1/public/content-manager/documents?directions=448
- Dataset ID: gov.kz-statistical-bulletin-listing,sheet=табл 4
- Unit: million KZT, year-to-date cumulative
- Frequency: irregular (year-to-date cumulative, roughly monthly)
- Period covered: 2025-02-28 .. 2026-07-31
- Methodology: Built from 13 of 13 listed 'Statistical bulletin' documents (0 skipped -- missing sheet or unparseable period/row). Million KZT, republican budget only. YEAR-TO-DATE CUMULATIVE, resets near zero each January -- do not read a January value as a sudden collapse from the prior December's much larger cumulative figure; that is the expected pattern for a cumulative-since-Jan-1 series, not a data error. No document currently covers a full Jan-Dec year; real gaps exist and are not fabricated/interpolated. Document TITLES ('as of {Month} 1') are NOT used to determine period -- they were found unreliable; only each document's own internal period text is trusted. 1 earlier point(s) restored on 2026-09-25 from bulletins archived in data/raw/minfin (no longer in the gov.kz listing).
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

Document TITLES ('as of {Month} 1') are NOT used to determine period -- they were found unreliable; only each document's own internal period text is trusted. 1 earlier point(s) restored on 2026-09-25 from bulletins archived in data/raw/minfin (no longer in the gov.kz listing).

## OIL_EXPORTS_VOLUME
- Name: Экспорт сырой нефти, объём / Crude Oil Exports, Volume
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/api/iblock/element/446905/file/ru/
- Dataset ID: EXPORTS_VOLUME_BY_COMMODITY_GROUP[CRUDE_OIL]
- Unit: tonnes
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2015-01-01 .. 2026-07-01
- Methodology: Derived from the item-level dataset EXPORTS_VOLUME_BY_COMMODITY_GROUP, item CRUDE_OIL. Same groups as EXPORTS_VALUE_BY_COMMODITY_GROUP. Natural gas (271121) is published in cubic metres only — its tonnes are zero here. Raw file: bns_exports_2026-09-16.xlsx.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## OIL_EXPORTS_VALUE
- Name: Экспорт сырой нефти, стоимость / Crude Oil Exports, Value
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/api/iblock/element/446905/file/ru/
- Dataset ID: EXPORTS_VALUE_BY_COMMODITY_GROUP[CRUDE_OIL]
- Unit: thousand USD
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2015-01-01 .. 2026-07-01
- Methodology: Derived from the item-level dataset EXPORTS_VALUE_BY_COMMODITY_GROUP, item CRUDE_OIL. TOTAL is the published national row; the groups are sums of 6-digit HS lines over the regional blocks. Gas condensate and the 10-digit 'основные товары' subsets (petroleum products) cannot be separated at 6 digits — CRUDE_OIL is all of 2709, PETROLEUM_PRODUCTS all of 2710. Raw file: bns_exports_2026-09-16.xlsx.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## OIL_PRODUCTION
- Name: Добыча нефти, включая газовый конденсат / Crude Oil Production including Gas Condensate
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/business-statistics/stat-industrial-production/
- Dataset ID: osnovnye-pokazateli-promyshlennosti/sheet-3/neft-vklyuchaya-kondensat
- Unit: thousand tonnes
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2025-05-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Thousand tonnes. Crude oil including gas condensate, from the monthly BNS publication 'Основные показатели работы промышленности', sheet 3 (output in physical terms). Each edition reports one month, and the site keeps only about three editions online, so this series is ACCUMULATED across runs and grows month by month. Element ids are resolved from the page each run rather than hardcoded, because editions are republished under new ids.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## INDUSTRIAL_OUTPUT
- Name: Объём промышленного производства / Industrial Output
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/business-statistics/stat-industrial-production/
- Dataset ID: osnovnye-pokazateli-promyshlennosti/sheet-2/Промышленность
- Unit: million KZT
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2026-04-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Million KZT. Value of industrial output (goods and services), all industry. Monthly and current, unlike the existing IND_PROD, which is an annual index from the stat.gov.kz cubes and stops at 2023. Accumulated across runs because the source keeps only about three monthly editions online.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## INDUSTRIAL_PRODUCTION_INDEX
- Name: Индекс промышленного производства (г/г) / Industrial Production Index (year-on-year)

- Dataset ID: osnovnye-pokazateli-promyshlennosti/sheet-2/Промышленность
- Unit: index, same month previous year = 100
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2026-05-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Index, same month of the previous year = 100. The monthly, current replacement for the annual IND_PROD series, which stops at 2023. One point per edition, since the sheet publishes an index only for the reporting month.

- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

- Unit: thousand KZT, year-to-date cumulative
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2026-03-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Thousand KZT, YEAR-TO-DATE CUMULATIVE (not a monthly flow) -- the sheet reports against the same PERIOD of the previous year. Replaces the annual INVESTMENT series, which stops at 2022. Accumulated across runs because the source keeps only a handful of editions online. 11.46 trillion KZT for January-July 2026, about 19.6 trillion a year.

## EXPORT_PRICE_INDEX
- Name: Индекс цен экспортных поставок / Export Price Index
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/economy/prices/
- Dataset ID: indeks-cen-vneshney-torgovli/sheet-1/Экспорт - всего
- Unit: index, same month previous year = 100
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2026-06-01 .. 2026-07-01
- Methodology: Index, same month of the previous year = 100. Price of Kazakhstan's goods exports. The dataset previously held only physical VOLUME indices for trade, so nominal moves could not be split into price and quantity. Read against IMPORT_PRICE_INDEX for terms of trade across all goods -- broader than COMMODITY_TERMS_OF_TRADE, which covers the commodity basket only. The source sheet also publishes six other comparisons on the same row (against last December, against December 2020, and three quarterly ones); this is specifically the year-on-year monthly one.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

- Frequency: monthly

- Methodology: Index, same month of the previous year = 100. Price of Kazakhstan's goods imports, on the same basis and from the same publication as EXPORT_PRICE_INDEX.

- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## STATE_TAX_REVENUE_YTD
- Name: Налоговые поступления государственного бюджета, нарастающим итогом с начала года, помесячно / State budget tax receipts, year-to-date, monthly
- Source: minfin, https://www.gov.kz/api/v1/public/content-manager/documents?directions=448
- Dataset ID: gov.kz-statistical-bulletin,sheet=табл 3,all pages
- Unit: million KZT
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2016-12-01 .. 2026-07-01
- Methodology: Million KZT, STATE budget (republican plus local) tax receipts, total, YEAR-TO-DATE cumulative (January to the month dated), from every Statistical Bulletin on every page of the listing (91 of 91 parsed). The latest edition wins where editions differ (10 revised points across the six series). Where an edition gave discrete quarters (2020 - mid 2021) they are cumulated to March/June/September/December. Months no edition covers are missing, not interpolated.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-25

## CUSTOMS_DUTIES
- Name: Таможенные платежи (республиканский бюджет) / Customs Duties (Republican Budget)
- Source: minfin, https://www.gov.kz/api/v1/public/content-manager/documents?directions=448
- Dataset ID: gov.kz-statistical-bulletin-listing,sheet=табл 8 (дох)
- Unit: million KZT, year-to-date cumulative
- Frequency: irregular (year-to-date cumulative, roughly monthly)
- Period covered: 2025-02-28 .. 2026-07-31
- Methodology: Built from 13 of 13 listed 'Statistical bulletin' documents (0 skipped -- missing sheet or unparseable period/row). Million KZT, republican budget only. YEAR-TO-DATE CUMULATIVE, resets near zero each January -- do not read a January value as a sudden collapse from the prior December's much larger cumulative figure; that is the expected pattern for a cumulative-since-Jan-1 series, not a data error. No document currently covers a full Jan-Dec year; real gaps exist and are not fabricated/interpolated. Document TITLES ('as of {Month} 1') are NOT used to determine period -- they were found unreliable; only each document's own internal period text is trusted. 1 earlier point(s) restored on 2026-09-25 from bulletins archived in data/raw/minfin (no longer in the gov.kz listing).
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

- Unit: USD
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2024-12-31 .. 2026-06-30

National Fund 'stabilization portfolio' (Стабилизационный портфель) sub-total -- the smaller, liquidity-focused portion of the Fund, held mostly in cash and money-market instruments. A POINT-IN-TIME snapshot as of each calendar quarter-end, not a cumulative flow (unlike most other minfin bulletin-sourced indicators). Originally sourced by Minfin FROM the National Bank RK, republished here in the Statistical Bulletin. Document TITLES are NOT used to determine period -- only each document's own internal 'ЗА N КВАРТАЛ YYYY ГОДА' header text is trusted.

- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

Point-in-time snapshot, not a flow. earlier points restored on 2026-09-25 from bulletins archived in data/raw/minfin (no longer in the gov.kz listing). 1 January 2025 = 434 785 as every 2025-Q1 and 2026 edition states it; the Jun-Nov 2025 editions put 508 222.9 (the 1 January 2024 figure) under that date and are not used for it.

- Methodology: Built from 13 of 13 listed 'Statistical bulletin' documents (0 skipped -- missing sheet or unparseable period/row). Million KZT, republican budget only. YEAR-TO-DATE CUMULATIVE, resets near zero each January -- do not read a January value as a sudden collapse from the prior December's much larger cumulative figure; that is the expected pattern for a cumulative-since-Jan-1 series, not a data error. No document currently covers a full Jan-Dec year; real gaps exist and are not fabricated/interpolated. Document TITLES ('as of {Month} 1') are NOT used to determine period -- they were found unreliable; only each document's own internal period text is trusted. 1 earlier point(s) restored on 2026-09-25 from bulletins archived in data/raw/minfin (no longer in the gov.kz listing).
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## RETAIL_TRADE_MONTHLY
- Name: Объём розничной торговли, помесячно / Retail Trade Turnover, monthly
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/economy/local-market/
- Dataset ID: statistika-vnutrenney-torgovli/sheet-1/Розничная торговля, всего
- Unit: thousand KZT
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2026-04-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Thousand KZT, single month (not cumulative). Retail sales of goods and services, whole country. The existing RETAIL_TRADE series carries the same concept ANNUALLY from the stat.gov.kz cubes; this is the monthly publication behind it. Accumulated across runs because the source keeps only a few editions online. Note the source sheet repeats this row label for rural areas alone further down -- this is the national figure.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## WHOLESALE_TRADE_MONTHLY
- Name: Объём оптовой торговли, помесячно / Wholesale Trade Turnover, monthly
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/economy/local-market/
- Dataset ID: statistika-vnutrenney-torgovli/sheet-1/Оптовая торговля
- Unit: thousand KZT
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2026-04-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Thousand KZT, single month. Wholesale sales of goods, whole country, from the same sheet as RETAIL_TRADE_MONTHLY. Wholesale runs roughly twice retail in Kazakhstan (19.6 against 9.0 trillion KZT over January-May 2026). Monthly companion to the annual WHOLESALE_TRADE.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## RETAIL_TRADE_INDEX_MONTHLY
- Name: Индекс физического объёма розничной торговли, помесячно / Retail Trade Physical Volume Index, monthly
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/economy/local-market/
- Dataset ID: statistika-vnutrenney-torgovli/sheet-2/Розничная торговля
- Unit: index (same month previous year = 100)
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2026-04-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Index, same month of the previous year = 100 -- retail volume in REAL terms, the consumption-side companion to RETAIL_TRADE_MONTHLY, which is in current prices. Monthly replacement for the annual RETAIL_TRADE_VOLUME_INDEX. The source row also publishes month-on-month and year-to-date comparisons; this is the year-on-year monthly one.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## WHOLESALE_TRADE_INDEX_MONTHLY
- Name: Индекс физического объёма оптовой торговли, помесячно / Wholesale Trade Physical Volume Index, monthly
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/economy/local-market/
- Dataset ID: statistika-vnutrenney-torgovli/sheet-2/Оптовая торговля
- Unit: index (same month previous year = 100)
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2026-04-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Index, same month of the previous year = 100. Wholesale volume in real terms, on the same basis as RETAIL_TRADE_INDEX_MONTHLY and from the same sheet, which carries a separate regional block per trade type.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## CONSTRUCTION_OUTPUT
- Name: Объём выполненных строительных работ / Construction Work Performed
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/business-statistics/stat-inno-build/
- Dataset ID: stroitelnaya-deyatelnost/sheet-1/Объем выполненных строительных работ/col0
- Unit: thousand KZT
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2026-04-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Thousand KZT, YEAR-TO-DATE CUMULATIVE (not a monthly flow) -- the cover names a January-to-month span and the sheet compares against the same PERIOD of the previous year. 5.06 trillion KZT for January-July 2026. Kazakhstan's construction is strongly seasonal and back-loaded, so seven months carry only about 40% of the annual total. The section also publishes annual and quarterly editions with the same sheet and row label; only editions whose cover states a monthly release interval are read. Accumulated across runs because the source keeps only a few editions online.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## CONSTRUCTION_INDEX
- Name: Индекс объёма строительных работ / Construction Work Index
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/business-statistics/stat-inno-build/
- Dataset ID: stroitelnaya-deyatelnost/sheet-1/Объем выполненных строительных работ/col1
- Unit: index (same period previous year = 100)
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2026-04-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Index, same period of the previous year = 100, year-to-date. The real-terms companion to CONSTRUCTION_OUTPUT, which is in current prices.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## LABOR_FORCE
- Name: Рабочая сила / Labour Force
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/labor-and-income/stat-empt-unempl/
- Dataset ID: 1. Основные индикаторы рынка труда/Рабочая сила, человек/col0
- Unit: persons
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2026-01-01 .. 2026-04-01
- Methodology: Persons aged 15 and over who are employed or unemployed, whole country. 9.84 million in Q1 2026. The denominator behind the unemployment and participation rates. Checked against the same sheet's employed and unemployed counts, which must add up to it.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## LABOR_FORCE_PARTICIPATION_RATE
- Name: Уровень участия в рабочей силе / Labour Force Participation Rate
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/labor-and-income/stat-empt-unempl/
- Dataset ID: 1. Основные индикаторы рынка труда/Уровень участия в рабочей силе/col0
- Unit: % of population 15+
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2026-01-01 .. 2026-04-01
- Methodology: Percent of the population aged 15 and over that is in the labour force. 67.2% in Q1 2026. Flagged by the sector audit as missing entirely: without it, a falling unemployment rate cannot be told apart from people leaving the labour force.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## EMPLOYED_QUARTERLY
- Name: Занятое население, поквартально / Employed Population, quarterly
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/labor-and-income/stat-empt-unempl/
- Dataset ID: 1. Основные индикаторы рынка труда/Занятое население, человек/col0
- Unit: persons
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2026-01-01 .. 2026-04-01
- Methodology: Persons. Quarterly companion to the annual EMPLOYED_TOTAL. 9.39 million in Q1 2026, of whom 7.29 million are employees and 2.10 million self-employed -- a split that matters in Kazakhstan, where self-employment absorbs much of what would otherwise be unemployment.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## UNEMPLOYED_PERSONS
- Name: Безработное население / Unemployed Population
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/labor-and-income/stat-empt-unempl/
- Dataset ID: 1. Основные индикаторы рынка труда/Безработное население, человек/col0
- Unit: persons
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2026-01-01 .. 2026-04-01
- Methodology: Persons. The count behind the existing UNEMPLOYMENT rate series, which is stale since 2025-06. 446,049 in Q1 2026.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## YOUTH_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE
- Name: Уровень молодёжной безработицы / Youth Unemployment Rate
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/labor-and-income/stat-empt-unempl/
- Dataset ID: 1. Основные индикаторы рынка труда/Уровень молодежной безработицы/col0
- Unit: %
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2026-01-01 .. 2026-04-01
- Methodology: Percent. Unemployment among people aged 15-34, as the source defines youth (Law «On State Youth Policy»). 3.0% in Q1 2026 -- below the headline rate of 4.5%, the reverse of the usual pattern in most economies.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## LONG_TERM_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE
- Name: Уровень долгосрочной безработицы / Long-term Unemployment Rate
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/labor-and-income/stat-empt-unempl/
- Dataset ID: 1. Основные индикаторы рынка труда/Уровень долгосрочной безработицы/col0
- Unit: %
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2026-01-01 .. 2026-04-01
- Methodology: Percent. Share of the labour force unemployed for a year or more. 1.6% in Q1 2026, about a third of total unemployment -- the structural component of the headline rate.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## AVG_WAGE_QUARTERLY
- Name: Среднемесячная номинальная заработная плата, поквартально / Average Monthly Nominal Wage, quarterly
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/labor-and-income/stat-wags/
- Dataset ID: 1. Численность наемных работников/Всего/col3
- Unit: KZT
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2026-01-01 .. 2026-01-01
- Methodology: KZT per month, including small enterprises. Quarterly companion to the annual AVG_WAGE. 445,068 KZT in Q1 2026, against 443,315 KZT for all of 2025 in the annual series -- two independently sourced series meeting where they should.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## REAL_WAGE_INDEX_QUARTERLY
- Name: Индекс реальной заработной платы, поквартально / Real Wage Index, quarterly
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/labor-and-income/stat-wags/
- Dataset ID: 1. Численность наемных работников/Всего/col7
- Unit: index (same quarter previous year = 100)
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2026-01-01 .. 2026-01-01
- Methodology: Index, same quarter of the previous year = 100. Wages after inflation. 99.8 in Q1 2026 -- nominal wages up 11.5% year on year but real wages flat, which is the number that matters for consumption. The source row also publishes a quarter-on-quarter version; this is the year-on-year one.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## FREIGHT_TURNOVER_MONTHLY
- Name: Грузооборот, помесячно / Freight Turnover, monthly
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/business-statistics/stat-transport/
- Dataset ID: pokazateli-raboty-transporta/sheet-1/vsego/col3
- Unit: million tonne-km
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2026-05-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Million tonne-km, YEAR-TO-DATE CUMULATIVE, all modes. Monthly companion to the annual FREIGHT_TURNOVER, and it reconciles with it: 298,327.53 mln t-km for January-July 2026 annualises to 511 bn against 512.6 bn reported for 2025. Note the source stores these cells as TEXT rather than numbers.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## PASSENGER_TURNOVER_MONTHLY
- Name: Пассажирооборот, помесячно / Passenger Turnover, monthly
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/business-statistics/stat-transport/
- Dataset ID: pokazateli-raboty-transporta/sheet-1/vsego/col7
- Unit: million passenger-km
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2026-05-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Million passenger-km, YEAR-TO-DATE CUMULATIVE, all modes. DO NOT SPLICE THIS ONTO THE ANNUAL PASSENGER_TURNOVER SERIES. That series ends in 2016 at 266.8 bn p-km, while this one reads 54,627.9 mln p-km for January-July 2026, or about 94 bn annualised -- roughly a third of the old level. The break is unexplained: nothing published in this section resolves it, since the long-run archive there covers passengers CARRIED, a different indicator. Kept as a separate series and flagged rather than smoothed over.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## FREIGHT_CARRIED
- Name: Перевезено грузов / Freight Carried
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/business-statistics/stat-transport/
- Dataset ID: pokazateli-raboty-transporta/sheet-1/vsego/col1
- Unit: thousand tonnes
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2026-05-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Thousand tonnes, YEAR-TO-DATE CUMULATIVE, all modes. Physical tonnage, the companion to FREIGHT_TURNOVER_MONTHLY, which weights tonnage by distance. Read together they separate how much moved from how far it moved.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## PASSENGERS_CARRIED
- Name: Перевезено пассажиров / Passengers Carried
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/business-statistics/stat-transport/
- Dataset ID: pokazateli-raboty-transporta/sheet-1/vsego/col5
- Unit: thousand people
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2026-05-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Thousand people, YEAR-TO-DATE CUMULATIVE, all modes. The count companion to PASSENGER_TURNOVER_MONTHLY.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## GINI_COEFFICIENT
- Name: Коэффициент Джини / Gini Coefficient
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/labor-and-income/stat-life/spreadsheets/?name=18651&year=YYYY
- Dataset ID: bns/18651 «Основные показатели бедности» + taldau/704502
- Unit: coefficient (0-1)
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2011-01-01 .. 2026-01-01
- Methodology: Gini coefficient of income, computed over 10% (decile) population groups. 0.283 for Q1 2026 -- low by international standards. The dataset held POVERTY_HEADCOUNT but no measure of how income is DISTRIBUTED, so a falling poverty rate could not be told apart from a widening gap above the poverty line. The source also publishes a quintile-based Gini (0.269 for the same quarter); this is the decile one, the finer partition. 15 editions; Taldau 704502 for the earlier periods, differing from the editions on 0 overlapping points.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-25

## DECILE_INCOME_RATIO
- Name: Соотношение доходов 10% наиболее и наименее обеспеченных / Decile Income Ratio
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/labor-and-income/stat-life/spreadsheets/?name=18651&year=YYYY
- Dataset ID: bns/18651 «Основные показатели бедности»
- Unit: ratio
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2022-07-01 .. 2026-01-01
- Methodology: Ratio of the income of the richest 10% to the poorest 10%. 5.66 in Q1 2026. Reads the same distribution as GINI_COEFFICIENT but at the tails, where the Gini is least sensitive. 15 editions.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-25

## GINI_COEFFICIENT_ANNUAL
- Name: Коэффициент Джини (по 10% группам населения), годовой / Gini Coefficient (decile groups), annual
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/labor-and-income/stat-life/spreadsheets/?name=18651&year=YYYY
- Dataset ID: bns/18651 «Основные показатели бедности» + taldau/704502
- Unit: coefficient (0-1)
- Frequency: annual
- Period covered: 2001-12-31 .. 2025-12-31
- Methodology: Gini coefficient of money income over decile groups, annual, from 2001 (Taldau 704502; from 2022 the annual editions of «Основные показатели дифференциации доходов», which equal Taldau). 0.291 in 2025. 4 editions; Taldau 704502 for the earlier periods, differing from the editions on 0 overlapping points.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-25

## DECILE_INCOME_RATIO_ANNUAL
- Name: Коэффициент фондов (доходы 10% наиболее к 10% наименее обеспеченных), годовой / Decile Income Ratio, annual
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/labor-and-income/stat-life/spreadsheets/?name=18651&year=YYYY
- Dataset ID: bns/18651 «Основные показатели бедности» + taldau/704504
- Unit: ratio
- Frequency: annual
- Period covered: 2011-12-31 .. 2025-12-31
- Methodology: Income of the richest 10% over the poorest 10% (коэффициент фондов), annual: Taldau 704504 for 2011-2021, the annual editions from 2022. 5.98 in 2025. 4 editions; Taldau 704504 for the earlier periods, differing from the editions on 0 overlapping points.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-25

## POVERTY_DEPTH
- Name: Глубина бедности / Poverty Depth
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/labor-and-income/stat-life/spreadsheets/?name=18651&year=YYYY
- Dataset ID: bns/18651 «Основные показатели бедности»
- Unit: %
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2022-07-01 .. 2026-01-01
- Methodology: Percent. How far below the subsistence minimum the poor fall on average, weighted by the population -- 0.9% in Q1 2026. POVERTY_HEADCOUNT counts who is poor; this measures how poor, and the two can move in opposite directions. 15 editions.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-25

## POVERTY_SEVERITY
- Name: Острота бедности / Poverty Severity
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/labor-and-income/stat-life/spreadsheets/?name=18651&year=YYYY
- Dataset ID: bns/18651 «Основные показатели бедности»
- Unit: %
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2022-07-01 .. 2026-01-01
- Methodology: Percent. The squared poverty gap, which weights the deepest shortfalls most heavily -- 0.2% in Q1 2026. Read with POVERTY_DEPTH and POVERTY_HEADCOUNT as the standard Foster-Greer-Thorbecke set. 15 editions.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-25

## BOP_GOODS_BALANCE
- Name: Сальдо баланса товаров / Balance on Goods
- Source: nbk, https://data.nationalbank.kz/api/v1/data?formId=324
- Dataset ID: nbk-form-324/GOODS
- Unit: million USD
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2000-01-01 .. 2026-01-01
- Methodology: Million USD, quarterly. Exports minus imports of goods. Kazakhstan runs a goods SURPLUS (4,068.9 mln in Q1 2026) alongside a current account DEFICIT -- the deficit comes from primary income, not trade. Beware: this form uses the code 'Goods' twice, the second time for goods bought by travellers under Travel, which is 0.0 throughout; the two are told apart only by the instrument fields. Every fetch re-checks that goods + services + primary income + secondary income equals the current account. History: 80 quarters before 2020-04-01 from formId=481, equal to 324 on all 15 shared unambiguous quarters, identity checked.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-25

## BOP_SERVICES_BALANCE
- Name: Сальдо баланса услуг / Balance on Services
- Source: nbk, https://data.nationalbank.kz/api/v1/data?formId=324
- Dataset ID: nbk-form-324/SERVICES
- Unit: million USD
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2000-01-01 .. 2026-01-01
- Methodology: Million USD, quarterly. Services exports minus imports -- transport, travel, construction, business services. Persistently negative but small next to the income account (-219.1 mln in Q1 2026). History: 80 quarters before 2020-04-01 from formId=481, equal to 324 on all 14 shared unambiguous quarters, identity checked.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-25

## BOP_PRIMARY_INCOME
- Name: Сальдо первичных доходов / Primary Income Balance
- Source: nbk, https://data.nationalbank.kz/api/v1/data?formId=324
- Dataset ID: nbk-form-324/PRIMARY_INCOME
- Unit: million USD
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2000-01-01 .. 2026-01-01
- Methodology: Million USD, quarterly. Investment income and compensation of employees, net. THIS IS THE DRIVER OF KAZAKHSTAN'S CURRENT ACCOUNT DEFICIT: -5,956.5 mln in Q1 2026 against a goods surplus of +4,068.9 mln. It is dominated by profit repatriation by foreign investors in the oil sector, so it tracks oil earnings rather than domestic demand. History: 80 quarters before 2020-04-01 from formId=481, equal to 324 on all 14 shared unambiguous quarters, identity checked.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-25

## BOP_SECONDARY_INCOME
- Name: Сальдо вторичных доходов / Secondary Income Balance
- Source: nbk, https://data.nationalbank.kz/api/v1/data?formId=324
- Dataset ID: nbk-form-324/SECONDARY_INCOME
- Unit: million USD
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2000-01-01 .. 2026-01-01
- Methodology: Million USD, quarterly. Current transfers, net -- personal transfers, government transfers and other current transfers. The smallest of the four components (+98.7 mln in Q1 2026). History: 80 quarters before 2020-04-01 from formId=481, equal to 324 on all 14 shared unambiguous quarters, identity checked.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-25

## STATE_BUDGET_REVENUE_YTD
- Name: Доходы государственного бюджета, нарастающим итогом / State Budget Revenue, year-to-date
- Source: minfin, https://www.gov.kz/api/v1/public/content-manager/documents?directions=448
- Dataset ID: gov.kz-statistical-bulletin-listing,sheet=табл 3
- Unit: million KZT
- Frequency: irregular (year-to-date cumulative, roughly monthly)
- Period covered: 2025-02-28 .. 2026-07-31
- Methodology: Built from 12 of 13 listed 'Statistical bulletin' documents (1 skipped). Million KZT, STATE budget (republican plus local), YEAR-TO-DATE CUMULATIVE -- resets each January, so a January value is not a collapse from the previous December. Read from column 5 of sheet 'табл 3'; the existing annual series of the same name reads column 3 of the same sheet. Total revenue. 15,036,307 mln KZT for January-June 2026 against 14,516,486 for the same period of 2025, both printed side by side on the sheet. 2 earlier point(s) restored on 2026-09-25 from bulletins archived in data/raw/minfin (no longer in the gov.kz listing).
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## STATE_BUDGET_EXPENDITURE_YTD
- Name: Затраты государственного бюджета, нарастающим итогом / State Budget Expenditure, year-to-date
- Source: minfin, https://www.gov.kz/api/v1/public/content-manager/documents?directions=448
- Dataset ID: gov.kz-statistical-bulletin-listing,sheet=табл 3
- Unit: million KZT
- Frequency: irregular (year-to-date cumulative, roughly monthly)
- Period covered: 2025-02-28 .. 2026-07-31
- Methodology: Built from 12 of 13 listed 'Statistical bulletin' documents (1 skipped). Million KZT, STATE budget (republican plus local), YEAR-TO-DATE CUMULATIVE -- resets each January, so a January value is not a collapse from the previous December. Read from column 5 of sheet 'табл 3'; the existing annual series of the same name reads column 3 of the same sheet. Total expenditure ('затраты'). 16,677,641 mln KZT for January-June 2026 -- spending ran ahead of revenue by 1.64 trillion before net lending and financial-asset operations. 2 earlier point(s) restored on 2026-09-25 from bulletins archived in data/raw/minfin (no longer in the gov.kz listing).
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## STATE_BUDGET_DEFICIT_YTD
- Name: Дефицит государственного бюджета, нарастающим итогом / State Budget Deficit, year-to-date
- Source: minfin, https://www.gov.kz/api/v1/public/content-manager/documents?directions=448
- Dataset ID: gov.kz-statistical-bulletin-listing,sheet=табл 3
- Unit: million KZT
- Frequency: irregular (year-to-date cumulative, roughly monthly)
- Period covered: 2025-02-28 .. 2026-07-31
- Methodology: Built from 12 of 13 listed 'Statistical bulletin' documents (1 skipped). Million KZT, STATE budget (republican plus local), YEAR-TO-DATE CUMULATIVE -- resets each January, so a January value is not a collapse from the previous December. Read from column 5 of sheet 'табл 3'; the existing annual series of the same name reads column 3 of the same sheet. Deficit (negative) or surplus (positive), -2,275,950 mln KZT for January-June 2026. Every fetch re-checks the budget identity against the latest bulletin: revenue - expenditure - net lending - balance on financial-asset operations must equal this line, and all five are published independently on the sheet. 2 earlier point(s) restored on 2026-09-25 from bulletins archived in data/raw/minfin (no longer in the gov.kz listing).
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## STATE_NON_OIL_DEFICIT_YTD
- Name: Ненефтяной дефицит бюджета, нарастающим итогом / Non-oil Budget Deficit, year-to-date
- Source: minfin, https://www.gov.kz/api/v1/public/content-manager/documents?directions=448
- Dataset ID: gov.kz-statistical-bulletin-listing,sheet=табл 3
- Unit: million KZT
- Frequency: irregular (year-to-date cumulative, roughly monthly)
- Period covered: 2025-02-28 .. 2026-07-31
- Methodology: Built from 12 of 13 listed 'Statistical bulletin' documents (1 skipped). Million KZT, STATE budget (republican plus local), YEAR-TO-DATE CUMULATIVE -- resets each January, so a January value is not a collapse from the previous December. Read from column 5 of sheet 'табл 3'; the existing annual series of the same name reads column 3 of the same sheet. The deficit excluding oil revenue -- -5,037,288 mln KZT for January-June 2026 against a headline deficit of -2,275,950. The gap between the two is what oil contributed to the budget, and it is the number that shows the underlying fiscal position. 2 earlier point(s) restored on 2026-09-25 from bulletins archived in data/raw/minfin (no longer in the gov.kz listing).
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

Read from column 5 of sheet 'табл 3'; the existing annual series of the same name reads column 3 of the same sheet. Budget loans issued minus repaid (255,162 mln KZT for January-June 2026). One of the two lines between the revenue-expenditure gap and the headline deficit; not previously in the dataset at any frequency. 2 earlier point(s) restored on 2026-09-25 from bulletins archived in data/raw/minfin (no longer in the gov.kz listing).

- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## EXCHANGE_RATE_CNY_KASE
- Name: Курс тенге к юаню на KASE / KZT/CNY Exchange Rate, KASE
- Source: nbk, https://data.nationalbank.kz/api/v1/data?formId=35
- Dataset ID: formId=35,currency=Chinese renminbi (yuan),type=Average exchange rate on KASE end of period (tenge)
- Unit: KZT per CNY
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2025-01-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Tenge per Chinese yuan, average rate on KASE at end of period. THE YUAN WAS ABSENT FROM THIS DATASET ENTIRELY -- no rate and no volume at any frequency -- although China is one of Kazakhstan's largest trade partners and the form carrying it was already being downloaded for USD volume. This is the KASE exchange rate, a different market from the EXCHANGE_RATE_*_OTC series and from the NBK official EXCHANGE_RATE.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## KASE_CNY_VOLUME
- Name: Объём торгов CNY/KZT на KASE / KASE CNY Trading Volume
- Source: nbk, https://data.nationalbank.kz/api/v1/data?formId=35
- Dataset ID: formId=35,currency=Chinese renminbi (yuan),type=Volume of trade on KASE for the period (units of currency)
- Unit: CNY
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2024-12-01 .. 2026-07-01
- Methodology: Yuan. Turnover of the CNY/KZT pair on KASE. Denominated in units of the foreign currency, like KASE_USD_VOLUME, so the four volume series are NOT comparable to each other without converting them first.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## KASE_EUR_VOLUME
- Name: Объём торгов EUR/KZT на KASE / KASE EUR Trading Volume
- Source: nbk, https://data.nationalbank.kz/api/v1/data?formId=35
- Dataset ID: formId=35,currency=Euro,type=Volume of trade on KASE for the period (units of currency)
- Unit: EUR
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2024-12-01 .. 2026-07-01
- Methodology: Euro. Turnover of the EUR/KZT pair on KASE -- the exchange, which is a different market from the over-the-counter FX_OTC_VOLUME_EUR. The two are not alternatives and must not be summed.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## KASE_RUB_VOLUME
- Name: Объём торгов RUB/KZT на KASE / KASE RUB Trading Volume
- Source: nbk, https://data.nationalbank.kz/api/v1/data?formId=35
- Dataset ID: formId=35,currency=Russian ruble,type=Volume of trade on KASE for the period (units of currency)
- Unit: RUB
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2024-12-01 .. 2026-07-01
- Methodology: Roubles. Turnover of the RUB/KZT pair on KASE, on the same basis as KASE_USD_VOLUME and KASE_EUR_VOLUME.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## PAYMENTS_TOTAL_COUNT
- Name: Количество платёжных транзакций, всего / Payment Transactions, total
- Source: nbk, https://data.nationalbank.kz/api/v1/data?formId=418
- Dataset ID: formId=418,payment_instrument=Total,period=Month,type=thsd. transactions
- Unit: thousand transactions
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2020-01-01 .. 2026-07-01
- Methodology: Thousand transactions. The count companion to PAYMENTS_TOTAL_VALUE -- value alone cannot separate people spending more from people transacting more often. 1.33 billion transactions in June 2026. NOTE the source's own Total is exceeded by the sum of its ten instrument rows on five of 71 months (2020-06, 2022-07, 2024-10, 2024-11, 2024-12); this is reported on stderr each run rather than silently reconciled, and the published Total is what is stored.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## PAYMENT_CARDS_COUNT
- Name: Количество транзакций по платёжным картам / Payment Card Transactions
- Source: nbk, https://data.nationalbank.kz/api/v1/data?formId=418
- Dataset ID: formId=418,payment_instrument=Payment cards, including:,period=Month,type=thsd. transactions
- Unit: thousand transactions
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2020-01-01 .. 2026-07-01
- Methodology: Thousand transactions on payment cards -- 97% of all payment transactions in Kazakhstan by June 2026 (1.295 of 1.329 billion). The count companion to PAYMENT_CARDS_VALUE. Splits into CASHLESS_PAYMENTS_COUNT and CASH_WITHDRAWALS_COUNT, which sum to it exactly.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## CASHLESS_PAYMENTS_COUNT
- Name: Количество безналичных платежей / Cashless Payment Transactions
- Source: nbk, https://data.nationalbank.kz/api/v1/data?formId=418
- Dataset ID: formId=418,payment_instrument=cashless payments,period=Month,type=thsd. transactions
- Unit: thousand transactions
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2020-01-01 .. 2026-07-01
- Methodology: Thousand transactions. Card payments for goods and services, as opposed to withdrawals. Against CASHLESS_PAYMENTS_VALUE this gives an average ticket of 12,600-13,600 KZT through the first half of 2026 -- about 27 US dollars, a normal retail purchase.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## CASH_WITHDRAWALS_COUNT
- Name: Количество операций снятия наличных / Cash Withdrawal Transactions
- Source: nbk, https://data.nationalbank.kz/api/v1/data?formId=418
- Dataset ID: formId=418,payment_instrument=cash withdrawals,period=Month,type=thsd. transactions
- Unit: thousand transactions
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2020-01-01 .. 2026-07-01
- Methodology: Thousand transactions. Against CASH_WITHDRAWALS_VALUE this averages 113,000-123,000 KZT per withdrawal -- roughly NINE TIMES the size of a cashless payment, which is the expected shape: cash is taken out in lumps and spent in small amounts. Read the two counts together for the cash-to-card transition rather than reading either alone.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## REMITTANCES_SENT_COUNT
- Name: Количество отправленных денежных переводов / Remittance Transactions Sent
- Source: nbk, https://data.nationalbank.kz/api/v1/data?formId=412
- Dataset ID: formId=412,currency_code=Total,money_transfer_sign=money transfers sent,period=Month,type=thsd. transactions
- Unit: thousand transactions
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2021-09-01 .. 2026-07-01
- Methodology: Thousand transactions, monthly, 2021-09 onward. The count companion to REMITTANCES_SENT. This is the form's own all-currency total: the per-currency rows carry a currency_code field and are excluded by requiring every other classification field to be empty, so no summing across currencies is done here.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## REMITTANCES_RECEIVED_COUNT
- Name: Количество полученных денежных переводов / Remittance Transactions Received
- Source: nbk, https://data.nationalbank.kz/api/v1/data?formId=412
- Dataset ID: formId=412,currency_code=Total,money_transfer_sign=money transfers received,period=Month,type=thsd. transactions
- Unit: thousand transactions
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2021-09-01 .. 2026-07-01
- Methodology: Thousand transactions, monthly. The count companion to REMITTANCES_RECEIVED. Kazakhstan sends far more transfers than it receives -- 160,313 against 44,854 thousand transactions in June 2026 -- so the two are not symmetric and should not be netted casually.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## CORE_CPI_YOY_EX3
- Name: Базовая инфляция без трёх составляющих, год к году / Core CPI ex-3, year-on-year
- Source: bns, https://taldau.stat.gov.kz/ru/NewIndex/GetIndexTreeData
- Dataset ID: taldau-index-55056856
- Unit: index (same month previous year = 100)
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2023-01-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Index, same month of the previous year = 100. Core inflation excluding fruit and vegetables, petrol and coal -- 110.7 for August 2026, so 10.7%. MONTHLY (the Taldau query uses the monthly period; an earlier note here said quarterly, corrected 2026-09-25). Sits just below the headline CPI_YOY, which is the expected relationship since the excluded items are the volatile ones.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## CORE_CPI_MOM_EX3
- Name: Базовая инфляция без трёх составляющих, месяц к месяцу / Core CPI ex-3, month-on-month
- Source: bns, https://taldau.stat.gov.kz/ru/NewIndex/GetIndexTreeData
- Dataset ID: taldau-index-55056856
- Unit: index (previous month = 100)
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2023-01-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Index, previous month = 100 -- 100.8 for August 2026. The momentum reading of CORE_CPI_YOY_EX3: it turns before the year-on-year series does, which is the point of carrying both.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## CORE_CPI_YOY_EX7
- Name: Базовая инфляция без семи составляющих, год к году / Core CPI ex-7, year-on-year
- Source: bns, https://taldau.stat.gov.kz/ru/NewIndex/GetIndexTreeData
- Dataset ID: taldau-index-55056857
- Unit: index (same month previous year = 100)
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2023-01-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Index, same month of the previous year = 100. The NARROWER core basket: on top of fruit, vegetables, petrol and coal it also excludes regulated utilities and rail transport -- the administered prices. 111.2 for August 2026 (monthly), slightly ABOVE the three-component measure, which says the administered prices were rising more slowly than the rest of the basket.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## CORE_CPI_MOM_EX7
- Name: Базовая инфляция без семи составляющих, месяц к месяцу / Core CPI ex-7, month-on-month
- Source: bns, https://taldau.stat.gov.kz/ru/NewIndex/GetIndexTreeData
- Dataset ID: taldau-index-55056857
- Unit: index (previous month = 100)
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2023-01-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Index, previous month = 100 -- 100.6 for August 2026. Momentum on the narrower core basket, the companion to CORE_CPI_YOY_EX7.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## AVG_WAGE_MINING
- Name: Среднемесячная заработная плата: горнодобывающая промышленность / Average Monthly Wage: Mining and Quarrying
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/labor-and-income/stat-wags/
- Dataset ID: 1. Численность наемных работников/Горнодобывающая промышленность и разработка карьеров/col3
- Unit: KZT
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2026-01-01 .. 2026-01-01
- Methodology: KZT per month. The highest-paying activity in Kazakhstan -- 1,020,632 in Q1 2026, 2.3 times the national average and 3.8 times agriculture. This is where oil sector pay sits, so it moves with commodity earnings rather than with domestic demand.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## REAL_WAGE_INDEX_MINING
- Name: Индекс реальной заработной платы: горнодобывающая промышленность / Real Wage Index: Mining and Quarrying
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/labor-and-income/stat-wags/
- Dataset ID: 1. Численность наемных работников/Горнодобывающая промышленность и разработка карьеров/col7
- Unit: index (same quarter previous year = 100)
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2026-01-01 .. 2026-01-01
- Methodology: Index, same quarter of the previous year = 100 -- 99.1 in Q1 2026. Real pay in the highest-paying sector was flat to falling even as the national figure held at 99.8.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## AVG_WAGE_MANUFACTURING
- Name: Среднемесячная заработная плата: обрабатывающая промышленность / Average Monthly Wage: Manufacturing
- Source: bns, https://stat.gov.kz/ru/industries/labor-and-income/stat-wags/

- Unit: KZT
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2026-01-01 .. 2026-01-01
- Methodology: KZT per month -- 508,854 in Q1 2026, half what mining pays. Read against AVG_WAGE_MINING for the pay gap between the resource and processing halves of industry, which is the same divide MANUFACTURING_OUTPUT and MINING_OUTPUT measure in volume terms.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-24

## RU_KEY_RATE
- Name: Ключевая ставка Банка России / Bank of Russia Key Rate
- Source: cbr, https://www.cbr.ru/DailyInfoWebServ/DailyInfo.asmx
- Dataset ID: DailyInfo/KeyRateXML
- Unit: % per annum
- Frequency: irregular
- Period covered: 2013-09-17 .. 2026-07-27
- Methodology: Percent per annum. One row per change of the Bank of Russia key rate, dated by the day it TOOK EFFECT (the decision is usually the business day before), from 2013-09-17 (5.5%). Collapsed from the daily series the web service returns.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-25

## RUB_USD
- Name: Официальный курс рубля к доллару США (ЦБ РФ) / Official RUB/USD Exchange Rate (Bank of Russia)
- Source: cbr, https://www.cbr.ru/scripts/XML_dynamic.asp
- Dataset ID: XML_dynamic/R01235
- Unit: RUB per USD
- Frequency: daily
- Period covered: 1998-01-01 .. 2026-09-25
- Methodology: Rubles per US dollar, the Bank of Russia's official rate, dated by the day it APPLIES to (set the business day before), from 1998-01-01 (after the redenomination). Since mid-2024 it is set from the OTC market (Moscow Exchange dollar trading halted). Accumulated across runs.
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-25

## RU_CPI_MOM
- Name: ИПЦ России, к предыдущему месяцу / Russia CPI, month-on-month

- Dataset ID: eec/CPI_1.xls/Россия
- Unit: index, previous month = 100
- Frequency: monthly
- Period covered: 2005-01-01 .. 2026-08-01
- Methodology: Index, previous month = 100: consumer prices, Россия, monthly from 2005-01, as published by the Eurasian Economic Commission from national statistics (one decimal, not seasonally adjusted).
- Transformation: level
- Revision status: original

## FOREIGN_DEMAND_YOY
- Name: Индекс внешнего спроса: рост реального ВВП партнёров, взвешенный по экспорту / Foreign Demand Index: export-weighted partner real GDP growth

- Unit: index, same quarter previous year = 100
- Frequency: quarterly
- Period covered: 2015-01-01 .. 2026-01-01

- Revision status: original
- Last updated: 2026-09-25
